Resident in Thiban and Tayyana were displaced to areas west of the Euphrates rives due to the clashes. Approximately seventy Tribal and Clan Forces remain in Thiban, with negotiations to secure their surrender ongoing. Conflicting reporting suggests QSD withdrew from several military points. 22 fatalities + 10 unspecified number of civilian fatalities. 32 fatalities coded.
